=== Note 1689519665257 (Cloze) ===
Tags: textbook:яблуко
Text: Vowel alternations and loss of final stem vowels in nominative singular nouns when declining:<br>- {{c5::Nominative plural}}<br>- {{c5::Genitive singular and plural}}<br>- {{c5::Animate Accusative singular and plural}}&nbsp;<br>- {{c5::Dative singular and plural}}<br>- {{c5::Instrumental singular and plural}}<br>- {{c5::Locative singular and plural}}<br><ul><li>In masculine and feminine nouns that end in {{c1::-і}} followed by one consonant, {{c1::-і}} changes to {{c1::-о-}} or {{c1::-е-}} in the genitive case.</li><li>In masculine nouns that end in {{c2::-ець/-ень}}, when adding the ending, we lose {{c2::-е-}}.<br></li><li>In masculine nouns that have the suffix {{c3::-ок}}, when adding the ending, we lose {{c3::-о-}}.<br></li><li>If there are changes after {{c4::л}}, then we add {{c4::-ь-}} after {{c4::л}}.<br></li></ul>

=== Note 1689520239145 (Cloze) ===
Tags: textbook:яблуко
Text: In the genitive plural forms of feminine and neuter nouns, if the last vowel of the nominative form is lost leaving a terminal consonant cluster, the vowel {{c1::-o-}} or {{c1::-e-}} appears between the cluster's consonants:<br><ul><li>If the cluster includes {{c1::к}}, the inserted vowel will be {{c1::-о-}}</li><li>If the cluster does not include {{c1::к}}, the inserted vowel will be {{c1::-е}}</li></ul>
Extra: цер<b>кв</b>а ⟶ цер<b>ков</b><div>&nbsp;крі<b>сл</b>а ⟶ крі<b>сел</b></div>


=== Note 1689520302600 (Cloze) ===
Tags: leech, textbook:яблуко
Text: In the genitive plural forms of feminine and neuter nouns, if the last vowel of the nominative stem is lost leaving {{c1::-о-}} in the last syllable, the {{c1::-о-}} becomes {{c1::-і-}}.
Extra: сл<b>о</b>во ⟶ сл<b>і</b>в

=== Note 1689520481192 (Cloze) ===
Tags: textbook:яблуко
Text: Feminine nouns that end in a consonant in the nominative singular form have the ending {{c1::-ей}} in the genitive plural form.
